# The external review traced to its resolution (2026-10-18)

The trace the backlog cites as "the 2026-10-18 trace of the review" (row "Operator output ports are defined twice: audit, a one-shot gate and each"): each item of the external review the owner gave on 2026-10-08 followed through its verification ([docs/reviews/2026-10-08-external-review-verification.md](../../../reviews/2026-10-08-external-review-verification.md)) to what main held at 8401820, by one agent for each cluster of findings and a completeness critic. It was kept only in the session's scratchpad under Temp (`C:/Users/arvik/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-arvik-code-byway/67b5b9ac-8595-4cc6-898a-d7d911b9d422/scratchpad/byway-review-to-resolution.md`). Claude brought it in on 2026-10-21. The text is unchanged; its headings are one level down, under this one, and a `<` that GitHub would read as the start of an HTML tag is written `&lt;`, so that it shows. The review itself, which the trace cites by line, is outside the repository, in the owner's review folder, and is not copied. The agents' working files are not kept.

## External review → verification → resolution

*byway, main at 8401820, 2026-10-18. Review: `byway_review/byway-review.md` (identical to the attached copy, SHA-256 2a889411…). Verification: `docs/reviews/2026-10-08-external-review-verification.md`. Traced by one agent per cluster plus a completeness critic; nothing in `byway_review` was modified.*

### Summary

- **The 26 numbered items** (F01–F20, R1–R5, RS): 3 partly, 18 root cause fixed, 4 decided and built, 1 filed with trigger. None is left without a disposition.
- **The review's other 140 recommendations** (its other sections and the reviewers' sub-reviews): 100 addressed, 17 filed with trigger, 5 unaddressed, 7 filed no trigger, 11 out of scope recorded.
- **Gaps found** by the completeness check: 25 (high 0, medium 7, low 18). They are listed at the end with proposed fixes.

**Verdict**

Nothing in the review is left without a disposition on main. But the claim that every item is resolved does not hold yet, for three reasons: two acceptance items in F01 and F02 are still open, about 35 residuals are filed with no trigger, and the fix status page is wrong in places.

**Coverage.** The inventory says it has 247 items, but its list has 312: 153 under F, 17 under R, 2 under RS, 70 X-items and 70 sub-review items.
- **F, R and RS items (172):** all map to the per-cluster traces.
- **The other 140, traced here:**
  - 100 are addressed on main, either by the F fixes or by research-track records that predate the review.
  - 17 are filed with a trigger. Most of these are harness requirements behind the 'Before the study registry's first entry' row, which label.test.ts trips.
  - 7 are filed with no trigger.
  - 11 are recorded as research-track scope.
  - 5 are unaddressed: dependency or security scanning (X-VAL-3, X-VAL-5, X-COV-2), the protocol map's Google Fonts (SUB-DA-8), and cancelling a large fan-out (SUB-DA-4).
- **Two inventory 'none found' entries are wrong.** F03.15 and F03.18 are addressed by ADR-0099 decision 7: a Start id stands for one Start, and reusing it with other inputs returns 409.

**Substance.**
- **The three P1 guarantees are closed on main with tests:** VOICE protection (ADR-0092 plus the run-time guard), drafts (ADR-0082) and exact copy (ADR-0081).
- **No traced finding is symptom-only, except F03's minor UI sub-cause:** the paid call during the GET render.
- **Two root fixes are incomplete:**
  - F01: the audit, gate and each port semantics are still defined twice, with no executed agreement test (G01).
  - F02: run inputs are still carried by value, and that row has no trigger (G04).

**Record-keeping.**
- **The status page's 'What remains: —' is untrue for 14 rows.** It also miscites ADR-0094 for R1 and RS, and undercounts F01's owner questions (G06).
- **ADR index links are missing, and R3's own trigger has fired unacted (G07).**
- **Decision evidence that Accepted ADRs rely on lives only in %TEMP%, under a trigger nobody can observe (G03).**
- **About ten rows wait on a 'first live run' that has no trigger, and live C5 is gated on rows with no trigger, which is circular (G05).**

**What to do first.** None of this is high severity, so the work is closing the record rather than re-fixing defects:
1. Correct the status page and the ADR links.
2. Copy the Temp evidence into the repository now.
3. Add the F01 operator-agreement test and the keepsVoice activity-level test.
4. Give the untriggered rows observable triggers.
5. File G02, which matters for the planned Claude Code channel because it would add a new caller of Runner.start.

#### F01: F01 — Make executable semantics authoritative (byway-review.md lines 124, 145-157; docs-advisor-review.md A1 and A3; engine-protocol-review.md E6)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P1 (review P1); the prompt binding part P2; the operator and gate-feed mismatches P3. (28, 61, 76, 110-176, 236-563 (F01 at 132-176; how it resolves F01 at 509-515; final-check residuals at 563-568))
- **Root cause:** Admission and execution use two definitions of what a recipe does. Admission reads self-declared annotations (writes, the 'voice' port, block labels) and structural approximations ('always runs' = not in an optional or route; a flow walk that runs every loop body to its end). It ignores variant, containers, gate feeds, loop exit points and the operators that put another version in place of the work. Execution derives all of these from actor, role, produces artifact, medium, variant and the enclosing operator, and nothing requires a declaration to agree with execution (verification lines 112-129, 170-176).
- **Wider than reviewed:** (2) the voice source is a declaration (V1, V1b); (3) a pick, keep-or-revert or scored loop after VOICE puts back earlier work (Q1-Q3, QA, QRh, QS); (4) checkpoints inside containers count (V2a, V2b); (5) checkpoints are counted by existence, not one to one or by variant scope (V2c, V3, V3c); (7) gates that cannot see their critic, and a C pick whose judge is a builder takes 'A' after a paid call; (8) VOICE identity readers disagree (X3, X4); (9) 'always runs' is structural and ignores loop exit points (K1, R1); (10) keepsVoice is checked only against the latest VOICE (K2b, K2f); (11) block labels decide DR7 (K2g). Placement oracle: 72 of 3,453 single moves break a protection today. The stored ok is never re-checked at start (lines 138-162).
- **Refuted or overstated:** (a) The runner-entry bypass is overstated: only stored ok:true composed recipes or catalog recipes start, so the real gap is that ok is never re-checked. (b) 'Reject disguised writers in audits' holds for human writes and VOICE, not for contained model writers such as vanilla-overlap, whose output is discarded. (c) Two citations are imprecise (compose.ts:95, profile.ts:99) (line 164).
- **Resolution (partly):** The P1 root cause is removed and guarded. @byway/recipe/semantics.ts defines the executed semantics once: the effects table, personWrite/voiceWrite/authorWrite, voiceScope, BLOCK_OF_ROLE, exitPoints/runsEveryTime, gateFeed and FIELD_ROLES. The validator (ST12 writer-undeclared/voice-*/block-role/set-aside/pick-*, PR6 on executed voice, ST9/ST10 human-write, scope set-aside), the composed profile (COMP-1 over runsEveryTime, a writer allowance per VOICE, COMPOSED-PROMPT by output contract plus a dry render with slotsForStep, and from ADR-0097 promptShows) and web start (Advisor.admit re-admits the stored recipe: 400 or 409) read it. The interpreter imports machineWrites, voiceWrite and feedsGate. A run-time PR6 guard (interpreter.ts:291) stops any machine write after the person's words that lacks keepsVoice, keepsVoice checks every held VOICE (ports.held), and a C pick whose judge names no draft fails instead of taking A. Agreement tests hold the module to the runner (parserFor, human writes, loop exits), and corpus tests (declaration monotonicity 3,952 changes, placement soundness 3,453 moves) guard regressions. All of the review's probes, re-run on main in my clone, are refused (the C4 hidden writer gives PR6, ST12 writer-undeclared and COMPOSED-VOICE; both prompt swaps give COMPOSED-PROMPT). What is only partly done: (1) the operator port disagreements (audit, one-shot gate, each) were settled by aligning validator/src/scope.ts (AUDIT_OUT, its gate case) to the interpreter. These semantics still live in two places, not in the shared module. No executed test compares the interpreter's audit, gate and each outputs with scope typing, which is the review's explicit acceptance item. A mutation in my clone that dropped the critique from the interpreter's audit and gate outputs failed only the bundle-hash pin in build.test.ts across 1,272 unit tests (e2e not run). (2) Set-aside (a pick, keep-or-revert or scored loop after the person's words) is enforced only at admission. Runner.start and the workflow run no product policy and no run-time set-aside, by a deliberate, delegated choice in ADR-0092, so a direct Runner.start of an unadmitted recipe is protected only by the PR6 guard and the held voice.
- **Records:** ADR-0092 (stage 1: semantics module, declaration rule, ST12, COMP-1, COMPOSED-PROMPT, Advisor.admit, protocol side-effect free), ADR-0098 decisions 7-9 (operator port semantics, the held voice at run time, the PR6 guard, the C pick throw, flow memo) and the answer-reach rules, ADR-0097 (a step takes in only what its prompt shows; COMPOSED-PROMPT on unshown ports), ADR-0093 (gate feeds, LS1), ADR-0100 item 14 (placement tally corrected: 1,093 of 3,453 admitted), docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md row F01

#### F02: F02 — Bound total work before constructing it (byway-review.md lines 125, 159-167; docs-advisor-review.md A2)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P2 (review P1). (29, 61, 76, 179-197, 336-340, 393, 401-402, 430-432, 494, 500-501, 516-522, 565-566)
- **Root cause:** Nothing at any admission layer bounds the counts that shape work (replicate n, loop max, relay maxLinks, lens count, nesting depth and size), and nothing says where a gate's retry may sit. The schema accepts any int, the validator sets only lower bounds, the profile checks only knobs, Runner.start only parses, and the interpreter expands eagerly. Batch bytes are width x per-activity input, and every activity input carries run-level and path-level data by value: seeds and option lists grow quadratically with the counts, and run inputs are set by the person. The count-determined part belongs in the engine's bound; the root fix for both parts is carrying that data by reference, a separate item (lines 195-197).
- **Wider than reviewed:** Ordinary counts already wedge runs: n=2,500 or nested 50x50 gives PendingActivitiesLimitExceeded; n=1,500 gives GrpcMessageTooLarge from RunRef.seeds by value (quadratic). A run stays 'running', Cancel can be ineffective, and $0 is spent. G1: a retrying one-shot gate inside replicate/each/audit throws RetryStage out of Promise.all without cancelling siblings, which keep making paid calls. About 1,000 nested seqs crash admission with an uncaught RangeError, because checkAnswer catches only ZodError (lines 183-189).
- **Refuted or overstated:** Memory exhaustion is overstated (the 5 s isolate deadline fires first). 'Apparently checked' is overstated (the diff shows the count). Severity drops from P1 to P2: nothing is spent, no human work is lost, and the person still starts a run whose count and estimate are shown (lines 189, 193).
- **Resolution (partly):** The count-determined root cause is removed at every entry. parseRecipe checks a shape bound iteratively before any recursive walk (64 levels of JSON, 10,000 op nodes, 1 MiB) and reports it as a ZodError, and checkAnswer turns any other throw into COMPOSED-INTERNAL. ENGINE_LIMITS (peak 500, 2,000 step runs, 3 gate retries, 2 MiB batch bytes counting every seed the run can hand out plus option ids and views) and workBound are enforced as ST3 'limit' at compose, by engineProblems at Runner.start (RecipeRefused before createRun) and inside run.ts at workflow start. ST10 'retry-scope' keeps a retrying gate out of fan-outs (G1). COMPOSED_LIMITS (n 16, loop 12, relay 8, lenses 4, peak 32, 400 runs, depth 12, 100 nodes, 32 KiB) cover model-authored recipes. The review's 1e9 probe, re-run on main, gives ST3:limit and COMPOSED-LIMIT, and n=17 gives COMPOSED-LIMIT. Not done: the byte half of the root cause the verification itself named. Run inputs (the brief with up to 5 x 200,000-character attachments, the fixed voice deck, ports.held and ports.voice) are still carried by value in every activity input, so canonical C1/C2 can still wedge in 'running' with very large attachments, the F02 failure mode. That item is in the backlog with no trigger. A runtime concurrency limiter was deliberately not built, replaced by the static peak bound at every entry. Prompt cancellation of a large valid plan is not separately tested.
- **Records:** ADR-0098 decisions 1-4 (shape bound, engine limits, work bound with measured byte constants, composed limits), ADR-0092 'Later stages: Stage 2', docs/backlog.md:69 and :86, docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md row F02
- **Tests:** packages/recipe/test/shape.test.ts; packages/recipe/test/limits.test.ts (loopRounds, ENGINE_LIMITS); packages/runner/test/limits-batch.test.ts 'workBound bounds every batch the interpreter schedules, and not by much' (four shapes incl. replicate(n, seq(seed, X))); packages/runner/test/start-refusal.test.ts 'Runner.start refuses what the engine cannot run, before a run exists' (1e9, C pick without judge, G1; createRun and start not called); packages/runner/test/e2e/loop-cap.ts 'the engine's own refusal at workflow start' (CI only here); packages/validator/test/limits-rules.test.ts; packages/advisor/test/limits-admission.test.ts (17 drafts, 50x50, 13 rounds, 9 links, 5 lenses, 33 peak, >400 runs, 1e9, G1); packages/advisor/test/semantics-admission.test.ts 'a pathologically deep recipe is a schema finding...(2,000 nested sequences)', 'a 2 MiB answer is a schema finding', 'COMPOSED-INTERNAL'; packages/catalog/test/limits.test.ts (every CATALOG recipe pinned; B-BoN N=1..64; N=125/126); apps/web/test/start-admission.test.ts 'the engine's own refusal at start' (400); Ran in clone: all of the above except the e2e passed; the review's 1e9 probe refused

| Claim | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| replicate 1e9 passes all checks | root-cause-fixed | ST3 'limit' (invariant; no exception relaxes it) and COMPOSED-LIMIT. Probe re-run on main: refused (ST3:limit x4, COMPOSED-LIMIT). limits-admission 'C1 with a billion seeded drafts'. |
| Eager Array.from/Promise.all before any budget decision | root-cause-fixed (by admission at every entry); eager expansion unchanged | The interpreter still expands eagerly, but engineProblems runs at Runner.start (client.ts:111-112) and inside run.ts:92 at workflow start, so no recipe past peak 500 / 2,000 runs / 2 MiB reaches the interpreter. start-refusal.test.ts. |
| Bounded worst-case size over nested replication, branches, loop retries, relay links | root-cause-fixed | workBound (limits.ts) multiplies replicate/each, sums audit lenses, counts C pick judge calls, loops at loopRounds.withRaise, relays by maxLinks, retries by times; limits-batch holds the byte bound to measured interpreter batches (at most 2x). |

| Small product defaults within tested hard ceilings | root-cause-fixed | COMPOSED_LIMITS for model-authored recipes, ENGINE_LIMITS for all kinds; catalog/test/limits.test.ts pins every recipe (C5 at 201 step runs, ADR-0098 decision 4). |

#### F03: Give each paid operation a durable identity and accounting lifecycle (byway-review.md:169-187; sources gateway-store-review.md GS-01, GS-03, GS-04 and engine-protocol-review.md E1)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY (verification §1 line 30; cluster B section line 602); severity P2 (review P1). Every leak is bounded or needs an unusual trigger: the cross-process overrun is limited to the workspace cap and one advisor estimate; the memo overlap needs a Temporal timeout or a dead worker; a torn ledger needs an OS crash, power loss or a full disk; a fal re-buy costs $0.35-1.70 per clip (lines 639-644). (Verification: §1 line 30; §2 line 62; §3 line 77; §5 line 98; cluster B root cause 571-597; F03 600-654; options 705-821; recommendation and design 823-1205; adversarial changes 1207-1274; final check and residuals 1276-1285. Review: byway-review.md:169-187, 375, 379-380, 424; gateway-store-review.md:23-37, 52-71, 111-113; engine-protocol-review.md:33-41)
- **Root cause:** Between admission and settlement the gateway kept no durable, shared record of a paid call. Exposure existed only as one process's in-memory reservation (gateway.ts:130) or as a settled JSONL line read leniently (ledger.ts:242). fal acceptance lived on the stack (fal.ts:127-129). The memo recorded results only after the effect (memo.ts:85-103). Nothing decided whether a failed attempt might have been billed: retries were blind, split between the SDK's hidden policy and a classifier that misread wrapped errors (lines 646-652). A separate, minor root cause at the UI edge: the GET render creates the recommendation as a side effect, and a run start carries no identity from the caller (line 654)
- **Wider than reviewed:** (a) The real re-buy paths: @fal-ai/client 1.10.1's hard-coded QUEUE_RETRY_CONFIG re-sends queue.submit 3 times on 429/502/503/504/network errors and overrides {maxRetries:0}, so the comment at fal.ts:69 was wrong; the gateway's withRetries re-submitted on 5xx; and a worker dying mid-clip led to a second submit (lines 620-625). (b) toGatewayError classed undici-wrapped resets as non-retryable (errors.ts:8-9, 61-67; line 623). (c) A torn tail made lastEntry() throw, so every later billed call failed and went uncounted (line 631). (d) verifyLedger() had no production caller (line 630). (e) Calls orphaned by a stop or a crash were uncounted (cluster root cause, lines 579-585). (f) Final-check residuals 1-7 (lines 1278-1284): a takeover race near lease expiry, under-specified key resolution, keys held 60 s after the invocation ends, an OS crash losing a ledger line already marked written, the drain's lock hold against the 30 s stale-lock break, unbacked 'none' billing assumptions, CJK reservations about 3x, and a waiter re-buying after the owner's memo saves fail.
- **Refuted or overstated:** The review's fal mechanism is wrong: toFailure maps every GatewayError to 'StepFailed', which acts.ts:12 lists as non-retryable, so Temporal never retries a poll-exhaustion error (lines 617-619). Memo race: the mechanism holds, but the trigger is narrow, and both calls are ledgered, so this is duplicate spend, not lost accounting (lines 611-615). A torn tail needs an OS crash, power loss or a short write; a process kill cannot tear the single appendFile (lines 632, 1254). UI identity is P3: Start is guarded while busy, and the duplicates cost cents and are ledgered (lines 634-637).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** The main root cause is gone on main. The spend book in byway.sqlite (contracts SpendBook, store migrations 6 and 7) is now the cap authority, shared by the web process and the worker. Each method is one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction, and the money transactions commit at synchronous=FULL. A call is admitted as open at its estimate, marked sent just before its first request, recorded as accepted with fal's request ids before polling, then settled at its true cost (idempotent), released (never billed) or abandoned as uncertain. A sent call that loses its lease becomes uncertain and counts against the workspace cap until the person clears it, so an expired lease never erases liability. GatewayOptions.spendBook is required, so no root can go back to per-process reservations (ADR-0095). One billingOf() classification, which walks err.cause, decides every retry. fal jobs are submitted once by REST POST with redirect:'error', and a 'maybe' submit is never re-sent (ADR-0091). Each step's paid call carries an operation key (run + memo key) held while its invocation lives. An overlapping attempt is refused in-flight and waits for the memo. A settled call answers settledEarlier, so the waiter looks for the kept result before it buys again. An accepted fal job whose holder is gone is resumed from its stored ids (ADR-0099). Ledger lines are absorbed idempotently into the book. A complete line that cannot be recorded becomes a fault that refuses paid admission until acknowledged. A torn tail is copied to ledger.jsonl.damaged-&lt;stamp>-&lt;pid> and the chain continues. Only the outbox drain appends, so a busy or failed ledger delays the entry, never the call or the caps. Run starts accept a validated client UUID, return the same run for the same inputs and give 409 otherwise (ADR-0099 decision 7). One exception: the minor UI sub-cause (paid work inside the Recommendation page's GET render) is fixed only at the symptom. Concurrent first loads share one advisor call per process (ADR-0091 decision 6), and the render can still call the model (page.tsx:47 -> recommendationOrProblem -> firstRecommendation). This follows the verification's own deliberate choice (line 1145), and it is filed without a trigger.
- **Records:** ADR-0091 (stage 1, provider edge), ADR-0095 (stage 2, spend book as cap authority), ADR-0099 (stage 3 operation keys; P3 run-start identity; web process stop), ADR-0100 items 2, 3, 13, 16, 17 (corrections to ADR-0083/0091/0096 sentences), ADR-0102 (D1; extends ADR-0099's keys to the decline retry), docs/backlog.md rows listed under residuals, docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md line 13
- **Tests:** packages/store/test/spend-book.test.ts: 'two processes admitting against one remaining budget (F03) > admit exactly as many calls together as the budget holds' (two child processes), plus the contract suite on two connections to one file; packages/contracts/test/spend-book-suite.ts (admit, sweep, markSent CAS, settle from every state, retain, absorb, faults, operation keys, resume); packages/gateway/test/spend-book.test.ts: 'two gateways on one spend book ... at $0.15, two $0.10 calls at once make one provider call'; 'a sent call whose owner died may have been billed ...'; 'a line in the middle that cannot be read stops paid calls until acknowledged; demo calls go on'; 'an entry appended before a crash could mark it is found again, not written twice'; one provider call' and 4 more; packages/runner/test/worker-loss.test.ts: 'a new worker resumes the clip by its stored request id ...'; 'the stopped seeds count against the workspace only ...'; packages/runner/test/restart.test.ts, ledger-faults.test.ts, restore.test.ts ('caps after a restore that lost the ledger (F03 ...)'); scripts/test/composition.test.ts (every root passes store.spendBook); Run in my clone at 8401820: 11 gateway/store files 339/339 pass, 8 runner files 61/61, 7 web/script files 66/66. (P3 double fault)' rows. The trigger on 'A counted re-buy when a call's holder dies ...' covers only the third-ask decline case.

So the status page's 'Named residuals in the backlog' is only partly true.
- ADR-0091's Negative 'A failed quarantine copy (a full disk) still fails the billed call, as before' has been untrue on main since ADR-0095: only the outbox drain appends (paid.ts:254-264), and a failed drain never fails the call. ADR-0100's corrections do not list it (the index does mark ADR-0091 as changed by ADR-0095).

#### F04: Separate cost estimates from enforceable caps (byway-review.md:189-197; source gateway-store-review.md GS-02)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY (verification §1 line 31; cluster B section line 659); severity P2 (review P1). The overshoot is limited to one extra attempt for each refused or sticky-routed call already in flight. It needs a classifier refusal, which is uncommon here, plus admission just below the cap. Later spend is refused because settlement is correct. Demo mode is unaffected (lines 690-696). (Verification: §1 line 31; §2 line 62; §4 D1 line 89; §5 line 98; cluster root cause 571-597 (esp. 593-595); F04 657-702; options 705-821; recommendation 823-841; stage 1c-1e 866-884; acceptance 1075-1079; F04 resolution 1184-1204; adversarial 1208-1221, 1251, 1255, 1259, 1268; final check 1276-1285. Review: byway-review.md:127, 189-197; gateway-store-review.md:24, 41-51, 118)
- **Root cause:** Two wrong assumptions (lines 698-702). (1) Admission assumed one billing plan, one attempt on the requested model. But the Anthropic adapter opted every request into a server-side refusal fallback, which can bill a second attempt, or a sticky-routed first attempt, on another model Anthropic picks. Settlement also silently substituted the requested rate for any served model with no price row. (2) The code and UI treated heuristic token estimates as guaranteed upper bounds ('estimates err high'), and an image of unknown size defaulted to 5000 tokens, below the code's own 6400 cap.
- **Wider than reviewed:** Sticky routing, which the review missed: for about an hour after a fallback, same-content requests are served directly by the fallback model (line 674). Corrected cost multipliers: up to about 2x on Sonnet 5.5, 2.25x on Opus 5.5, 1.5x on Fable 5.1 (line 673). The fallback was on by default, no ADR recorded it, and the research protocol requires it off (line 663). claude-sonnet-5 had no price row and was priced correctly only by coincidence (line 677). An unknown-size image defaulted below the max (line 682). Gemini image thinking tokens are ignored (google.ts:91; notDoing line 1144).
- **Refuted or overstated:** The overspend is neither silent nor continuing: the settled cost is the true one, and the next call is refused. The advisor's range only seeds the suggested budget and is not the enforced bound. The fallback fix does not need F03's lifecycle (lines 685-688). OpenAI and Google dated snapshots are correctly priced at the requested row (line 678).
- **Resolution (decided and built):** Root cause (1) is removed. ADR-0102 (owner D1 = 2b) removes every way to ask for a fallback. AnthropicAdapterOptions is { client } only. anthropicParams sends no beta header and no fallbacks field (anthropic.ts:40-72). GatewayOptions.anthropicRefusalFallback and REFUSAL_FALLBACK_BETA are gone. OpenRouter stays pinned with allow_fallbacks:false. A source guard over packages/*/src, apps/web/src, apps/web/app and scripts fails on any fallback request. So every dispatch bills the model it was admitted for, and sticky routing cannot happen. A decline gets one retry on the same model (askOnceMore in gateway.ts). The retry is a paid call of its own, admitted in the spend book against both caps, with its own operation key, and settled and ledgered with retryOf. Settlement prices each billed attempt at its own row. A served model with no row is priced at the requested row and flagged priceBasis 'assumed', which is recorded in the hash chain and shown in Settings (ADR-0091 decision 3). Root cause (2) is removed by contract (ADR-0091 decision 4; ADR-0102 decision 6). estimate.ts, spec §3.4, the README and the screens say planning estimates, not bounds. Non-ASCII text counts at its UTF-8 bytes. An unreadable image counts at 6400, and WebP sizes are now read (e3c4e6b). The UI says 'Estimated cost' and 'Estimates can be low ...'. A guard test keeps bound claims and fallback-overrun wording out. What remains is the estimate's own error: a call can settle above its reservation, the true cost is recorded, and later admission refuses against it. That is stated, not bounded.
- **Records:** ADR-0091 decisions 3, 4, 7 (7 replaced by ADR-0102), ADR-0095 decision 10 (replaced by ADR-0102), ADR-0102 (owner decision D1, option 2b), docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md line 14, docs/backlog.md rows listed under residuals, docs/playbook.md 'Protect the product's provider keys before the first live run' (provider-side spending limits, the review's 'provider limits' control)
- **Tests:** packages/gateway/test/declines.test.ts 'no model substitution, everywhere (ADR-0102)': 'the gateway has no switch that turns a server-side fallback on'; 'a Claude call asks for no fallback model: no fallback beta, no fallbacks field'; 'no source in the product asks any provider for a fallback model'; 'the source guard catches every form of a fallback request ADR-0102 names ...'; plus the retry, budget and outcome tests; packages/gateway/test/providers.test.ts: 'maps the request: ... and no fallback to another model (ADR-0102)'; 'has no option that asks for a fallback model (ADR-0102)'; packages/gateway/test/pricing.test.ts: 'servedPrice' table tests; 'flags a call served by a model with no price: priced at the requested row, priceBasis assumed ...'; 'prices a dated snapshot of a known model at that model's own row, unflagged'; packages/gateway/test/estimate.test.ts: 'counts 3000 CJK characters at 9000 tokens or more: their UTF-8 bytes'; 'counts a WebP (or any image) of unknown size at the 6400-token maximum'; WebP header tests; apps/web/test/guard-estimates.test.ts 'estimates are not bounds (F04)' (6 tests, including 'no screen, and not the README, still sizes the overrun by a fallback model that byway no longer asks for (ADR-0102)'); packages/runner/test/declines.test.ts, overlap.test.ts 'two attempts of one step's declined call (ADR-0102)'; packages/contracts/test/spend-book-suite.ts 'settles from every state, idempotently: the true cost wins ...' (overrun recorded, later admission counts it); Run in my clone at 8401820, all passing. Mutation check: adding fallbacks:'default' to anthropicParams failed 4 tests (2 in declines.test.ts, 2 in providers.test.ts)

**Gaps:**

- The backlog row 'Provider token counting instead of planning estimates', which holds F04's main remaining limit (estimate error, the gpt-image figure), has no trigger.
- The status page's F04 'What remains' column names only D1. It does not say that a call can still settle above its reservation by the estimate's error (ADR-0102 Decision 6), or that no-substitution is not enforced (backlog 'Served-model check in the product').
- The verification's acceptance test 'A C2 12-way seed stage at its suggested budget admits at least as many seeds concurrently as today (9 of 12)' (line 1076) is not present as a test. It is moot in practice, because reservations stay single-attempt under ADR-0102, and worker-loss.test.ts holds 12 seeds in flight.

#### F05: F05: Enforce script consent on full-size views (Full-size HTML serves a more permissive execution policy than its preview)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT: every claim holds at b33f1285; both verifiers reproduced the leak on a production build; severity P2 (review P1), first in the P2 order. Exploiting it needs hostile page content and a click, and only that one document's text (plus IP and timing) can leave. (Verification 1288-1296 (cluster root cause), 1298-1328 (F05), 1370-1460 (options), 1462-1472 (recommendation), 1476-1511 (Change 1 design), 1559-1581 (steps 1-7), 1601-1606 (acceptance tests), 1630-1645 (residuals, ADR-A), 1676-1682 (how it resolves F05), 1688-1726 (adversarial rounds and final check); section 1 row at line 32)
- **Root cause:** One URL (/artifacts/&lt;id>) serves one response policy to two viewing contexts. CSP sandbox flags intersect with the iframe's sandbox attribute, so for the per-page switch to work the route always granted 'sandbox allow-scripts', and consent lived only in the embedding iframe, never in the response. Any top-level navigation (Open links, fallbacks, Markdown links, typed URLs, history) got the scriptable policy, and the one-year immutable cache kept those responses alive. A second gap: the app CSP had no frame-src, so a framed page could be navigated off-site.
- **Wider than reviewed:** Seven in-app top-level paths, not three: also ResultView.tsx:107 'Open in a new tab' (next to 'Scripts are off'), the ArtifactPreview.tsx:55 fallback, the results.tsx:28 clip fallback, and model-written Markdown links (Markdown.tsx:18). A page with no JS leaked through meta refresh, because allow-scripts re-enables automatic features. The app CSP had no frame-src (frame-src probe). The immutable cache keeps pre-fix responses. Unit and e2e tests pinned the permissive header (server.test.ts:40-44, api.test.ts:199-201, configurations.spec.ts:57).
- **Refuted or overstated:** No factual claim was refuted; only the severity was lowered, P1 to P2. The review's repair as written (option A: route every view, in-app frames included, through one viewer route) was declined: ScaledPage and PageResult already are trusted viewers, so nesting frames adds nothing, and A does not close off-site frame navigation.
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Consent now lives in the response, not only in the frame. pageCsp(origin, scripts) in apps/web/src/server/artifacts.ts ends in a bare 'sandbox' unless pageScriptsGranted(url, headers) holds: ?scripts=1, no download, sec-fetch-dest iframe, sec-fetch-site same-origin. Any ask for scripts is answered Cache-Control no-store. artifactResponse (api.ts:427-455) takes request.headers from the route, and the media route never grants. The inert default never depends on the request, so every top-level path (links, typed URLs, history, future links) gets an inert page served after the fix. ScaledPage and PageResult take an id and derive src and sandbox from one boolean. Every in-app Open uses openHref → /view/&lt;id> (app/view/[id]/page.tsx), which shows a page in PageResult with the per-visit switch. A grep of src and app found exactly one place that builds the /artifacts/ path (api.ts:79). The app CSP in next.config.ts:62 has frame-src 'self'. Mutation checks in my clone confirm tests catch the cause returning. With the CSP set back to always 'sandbox allow-scripts', 4 of the 7 consent-boundary.spec cases fail (control passes; the frame-src cases need the second mutation) and 4 server/api unit tests fail. With frame-src 'self' removed, the 2 frame-navigation cases fail. Together that is 6 of 7, matching ADR-0085's claim. A later gap is closed: Markdown own-artifact links with ?query/#fragment opened the raw (inert) address until 730ebc7 (ADR-0100 item 7).
- **Records:** ADR-0085 (Decision, How it fails, Residuals, Evidence), ADR-0100 items 7 and 8 (Markdown query/fragment links; raw address also used by the whole-text loader and the viewer redirect), ADR index rows: ADR-0074 and ADR-0080 'changed by ADR-0085', apps/web/README.md consent bullet (lines 101-110), docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md row F05, Verification Cluster C, Change 1
- **Tests:** apps/web/test/server.test.ts: 'serves pages inert by default: a sandbox with no scripts and no same origin, and no network writes (F05)'; 'runs a page's scripts only for a frame that asked with consent, and never caches that answer (F05)'; 'grants a page's scripts only to ?scripts=1 asked by a same-origin frame (Fetch Metadata)...'; 'lets the app's frames show only byway's own addresses... (F05)'; apps/web/test/api.test.ts: 'runs a page's scripts only for ?scripts=1 asked by a same-origin frame, never cached; every other request is inert (F05)'; apps/web/test/open-links.test.ts: Open paths (StepDetail, PickPanel, ComparePanel, References, Result, ArtifactPreview fallback, Storyboard clip, Markdown incl. query/fragment); 'ScaledPage and PageResult derive the address and the sandbox from one switch'; scan 'has none in the app's screens and components'; apps/web/e2e/consent-boundary.spec.ts: 7 cases (control; top-level with/without ?scripts=1 and meta refresh; Open popup; scripts-off frame and its links; consented frame cannot leave; consented frame cannot hop to another page's ?scripts=1; self-reload fails closed). I ran them in my clone: 7/7 pass; apps/web/e2e/artifact-views.spec.ts: 'every Open goes to the viewer, with the page's scripts off (F05)'; 'the viewer's scripts switch (F05)' (toggle, reload resets, self-reload inert); top-level own address runs nothing; apps/web/e2e/configurations.spec.ts C1: raw address inert, ?scripts=1 without Fetch Metadata inert and no-store

ADR-0085 is delegated, not explicitly owner-approved | yes |

#### F06: F06 — Preserve unsent human work across inspection

- **Verification:** ACCEPT; severity P1 (review P1); at the P1/P2 boundary, kept P1 because the reset text can be sent at once as 'Your words' (verification:1740). (1729-1743 (cluster D root cause and F06), 1767-1840 (options), 1842-1903 (recommendation and design slice 1), 1919-1923 (examples, attachments), 1926-1932 (steps 1, 4-6), 1940-1948 (acceptance tests), 1976-1991 (not doing), 1995 (how it resolves F06), 2000-2033 (adversarial rounds and final-check residuals); verdict table line 33; cluster table line 64; adversarial summary line 79)
- **Root cause:** Person-authored input has no owner that outlives the component that shows it. Each panel keeps its draft in its own useState, seeded from the checkpoint, so the draft lives exactly as long as the YourTurn mount. Inspection, run end, a stale poll that drops the active checkpoint and a reload all unmount the panel, and the next mount re-seeds from cp.initialText. There is no checkpoint-keyed store above that boundary, no session persistence, and no rule that only server state settles a draft. The same assumption ties Start attachments and Recommendation examples to their mount (verification:1742, cluster root 1731).
- **Wider than reviewed:** The current-row toggle (RunScreen.tsx:226) and Esc (StepDetail.tsx:18) also unmount. Mod+Enter fires anywhere (useShortcuts.ts:44), so one keystroke sends the model's initialText as the person's VOICE (both verifiers captured the POST). Run end, a stale poll and a reload also unmount. Start attachments (StartForm.tsx:38) and Recommendation 'Examples you admire' (RecommendationView.tsx:58) have the same lifetime and are lost on the Settings detour that a budget refusal recommends (history.ts:60). F15 interacts: a stale snapshot unmounts YourTurn and wipes the draft (verification:1738, 1731).
- **Refuted or overstated:** Two items on the review's acceptance list already worked: polling and a failed POST keep the draft, because YourTurn is keyed by cp.id and stays mounted. These are regression checks, not current failures (verification:1738).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** The draft's owner moved from the panel to the Run screen. RunScreen.tsx:106-123 creates one createDraftStore(runId) per mount (the page keys RunScreen by run id, app/runs/[id]/page.tsx:24), above the work area's conditional render. A draft is keyed by checkpoint id. The kinds write, react, directive, pick and select go to sessionStorage through sessionSlot.ts (versioned envelope, every access guarded). The store is settled only by snapshots the refresher applied (the `served` flag, RunScreen.tsx:117-122), never by `initial` or by an unmount. All eight answering panels seed once from `draft` and write through `onDraft` (panels/*.tsx). A successful POST marks the answer sent, for every kind, which locks it read-only (draftStore.markSent; TurnFooter shows 'Sent'). Text left on a closed question is shown as UnsentWords, read-only with Copy and Discard and no send. YourTurn renders only once the drafts have loaded, so it never flashes initialText. The same helper keeps Start attachments (startDraft.ts) and Recommendation examples (examplesDraft.ts). The pre-merge re-check (22b0400) fixed two more defects test-first. Back without a reload had deleted the draft (settle now keeps a draft whose question is unlisted while the run goes on). The Sent lock had been editable (put now ignores changes to a sent draft).
- **Records:** ADR-0082 (Decision R1 and R3; Consequences; Evidence, including the 2026-10-08 re-check), ADR-0100 item 10 (held-key refusal: drafts.withhold keeps a refused answer in memory but not in the tab's storage), spec §5 Run, Start and Recommendation lines (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-product-v1-design.md), docs/backlog.md rows 'Your-turn drafts survive closing the tab' and 'Run screen on Back: hide Your turn until the first served snapshot', status page row F06: Fixed, 0082
- **Tests:** apps/web/test/drafts.test.ts (settle table, including 'keeps a draft whose question a snapshot does not list while the run goes on', 'shows text left on a question that closed unanswered … a run that ended with it still listed open', 'never restores into another question or another kind'); apps/web/test/draft-store.test.ts (StrictMode lifecycle, 'a pending write never brings back a draft that was sent, settled or discarded', 'while an answer is sent, changes to it are ignored', 'marks every kind as sent'); apps/web/test/panels.test.ts 'a panel opens with the draft the Run screen kept for its question (F06, ADR-0082)' (in my clone, mutating TextPanels to seed from initialText made 2 of these fail); apps/web/test/session-slot.test.ts, start-draft.test.ts, examples-draft.test.ts; apps/web/e2e/state.spec.ts:167 'what you type survives looking at earlier steps, Esc, the current row, a reload and Back, and is exactly what is sent'; state.spec.ts:192 'reached from Start with no reload … History then Back and Settings then Back'; state.spec.ts:214 'lists, ticks and highlights survive inspection; lists and ticks survive a reload, a compare highlight does not (UX-1)'; state.spec.ts:296 'a refused answer keeps your words; a sent one stays locked as Sent …'; state.spec.ts:362 'words typed for a question that closes unsent are shown read-only …'; state.spec.ts:387 'examples you admire and Start attachments survive a detour …'; e2e/keyboard.spec.ts:218 '[F06] with a question open, a step opened and closed by keyboard gives the turn back with your words'; All of these passed in CI run 39177268735 at main 8401820, on Linux and Windows; in my clone the 11 cluster-D unit files passed (115 tests)

#### F07: F07 — Preserve the person's exact final page-copy decision (byway-review.md:130, 221-229; sub-review engine-protocol-review.md E2, lines 43-51, where it was P2; the main report raised it to P1; byway-review.md:391 adds that the empty link is also an accessibility defect)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT. Every technical claim reproduces at b33f1285 (verification line 34, 2068-2076).; severity P1 confirmed (review P1; sub-review E2 had P2). Verification lines 2093-2108: P1 because no-model paths break the guarantee and canonical C5 is affected today. The RangeError alone would be P3; the last-line block alone P2 UX.. (Verdict row 34; adversarial summary 80; order 97; Cluster E 2036-2772 (root cause 2038-2063; F07 2066-2119; options 2122-2233; recommendation and design 2235-2470; acceptance tests 2473-2561; compatibility 2564-2599; ADR spec 2601-2632; not-doing 2635-2651; resolution per sub-claim 2654-2682; forced changes 2685-2754; final check and residuals 2757-2771))
- **Root cause:** Verification lines 2038-2047 and 2110-2119. The person's copy could be written or rewritten by something other than an exact, total application of their edit (a model, a lossy fallback, a reader that skips or misreads, a tokenizer that is not the browser's, a later machine writer whose lock scope is guessed or fails open), as long as the result passed a check weaker than the edit. So the runner never represented the person's decision exactly. The same weak predicate (lostVoiceLines) was the only lock on later machine writes, and ADR-0073 had recorded the weak page check as the design.
- **Wider than reviewed:** Verification lines 2078-2091: skeleton() ignores inserted text between tags; human.ts:161's squashed 'unchanged' test silently drops moves, merges and paragraph splits; human.ts:191 strips the trailing line, so the last block cannot be emptied; copyLines glues side-by-side links; unknown named entities are shown raw, then double-escaped; every non-web medium falls back to helper.voice.apply, checked only by substring membership, and not at all for an empty voice (writes.ts:20); keepsVoice uses the same substring check, and canonical C5's script phase reaches it (appended or reordered voice-over passes); parseOptionCopy and applyOnScreen drop or shadow typed lines; the tokenizer ends a tag at a '>' inside a quoted attribute; round 3's lock fails open on layout drift; svg CDATA and iframe fallback are offered as copy. Final check (2765-2771): duplicate shot numbers [1,2,2] make every changed late answer fail; coincidence c1; wording gaps (a)-(c); script-data escape states not modelled.
- **Refuted or overstated:** No review claim refuted. The verification withdrew its own round-3 example of a caption rewritten after the late VOICE: canonical C5 never reaches that path (2089, 2108). It also withdrew the parked 'second-pass cut fails' row (2241, 2695). It carved out VOICE-RETRY (C5's gate retry drops the late-VOICE copy) as a separate root cause, the workflow's stage re-entry, not a weak check (2063, 2385-2388).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Activity-only (the merge's diff of interpreter.ts, wording.ts, types.ts and prompts/src/ids.ts is empty, so the bundle is unchanged). VOICE no longer calls a model in any medium: human.ts no longer imports askModel, and the voice.apply id stays but is uncalled. Non-web decks: one total labelled reader (copy.ts readLabelled, which replaced parseOptionCopy and the lossy applyOnScreen), a deterministic applyCopy, and a round trip through one line-based layoutOf that never uses the reader. The same layoutOf is the 'unchanged' test (the squash and the trailing-line strip are gone). Pages: page-copy.ts reads a positional deck from a WHATWG-faithful tokenizer (html-tokens.ts; parse-entities, which is total and gives U+FFFD out of range), uses one bracket grammar for link words, places deterministically with a removal witness, and checks with a six-invariant verifier (pageEditProblems). Then the Chromium visitor read must agree (page-arbiter.ts, pages.ts shownText), or the answer is asked again. keepsVoice: lostVoiceLines is deleted. lockCopy/checkedWriter hold options, prose, code diffs and pages to exact deck equality (pages also hold each link's href). A storyboard's voice-over is written back, with a step-finished note, and its on-screen copy is locked when the late VOICE's own reader reads a held answer as the complete deck that is not the voice-over itself. Faults are typed: AnswerRejected for answers, a non-retryable StepError for the deck read and the lock. Every review reproduction, rerun against main's API in my scratch clone, is now written exactly or refused: deletion, repeats, swap, the plain-words link answer refused with the bracket rule, the bracketed link written as &lt;a href="/terms">the conditions&lt;/a>, and &#99999999; decoded to U+FFFD. Appended or reordered voice-over comes back with restored ['voice-over']; a page claim or duplicated heading after VOICE is refused. What remains is filed or accepted (see residuals): the raised-badge display case, the content-layer question, VOICE-RETRY, and the page lock not holding visibility or spacing.

#### F08: F08 — Explain actual composition changes (byway-review.md lines 131, 231-239; docs-advisor-review.md A4)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT; severity P2 (review P2; low P2). (35, 61, 200-214, 344, 385-389, 403-404, 436-437, 499, 523-527)
- **Root cause:** diffFromNearest is a hand-written comparer over a hand-picked subset of fields and node types, and its default is unsafe: 'same' means 'none of the compared fields differ', not 'semantically equal'. No shared definition says which fields, node types and placements affect execution, and the explainers read declared flags and a coarse pick mapping instead of executed semantics (line 214).
- **Wider than reviewed:** An added pick after VOICE, VOICE without the 'voice' port, VOICE turned into a claim-log or a vision step, C5's script VOICE without VOICE-early, C3's critic wrapped in an audit, C5's gate retrying D1, and changes to probe, plateau, a one-shot pass, optional triggers, sitsOut, consumes, produces, writes, keepsVoice and isolated all read 'The same steps'. Structural moves are under-described (V2a, K1-C1, K2b). present.ts calls every non-H pick 'The AI chooses', reads declared writes, and tells a claim-log VOICE 'write the words readers will take as yours' (lines 206-208).
- **Refuted or overstated:** The 'same admitted plan' part already holds: api.ts diffs and starts the same stored recipe. Citations are off (diff.ts:45 should be 37-38; diff.ts:183 should be 185-192) (line 210).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** The unsafe default is gone. sameRun compares semantic projections built from FIELD_ROLES, a mapped type over every schema object (an unclassified field fails typecheck, and a test holds it to the zod schema object by object). Names are dropped, sets sorted, and seq nesting and consumes/reads order kept. Every node type is paired. Picks ('chosen at random, no one compares them'), the gate bar ('stops at a lower quality bar', never quoted), early stops, VOICE scope, optional/route questions, reads, retries and moves across containers, exit points or past your words get specific lines, and any other projection difference reads 'Other technical changes that affect how it runs'. present.ts reads the effects table, voiceScope and appliesCritique (later promptShows) instead of declared writes. Web start re-admits and returns 409 if the stored explanation changed. Both review probes, re-run on main, now read 'Changed: Pick one (Refine): chosen at random, no one compares them, where you chose before' and 'Changed: Outside critique repeats: stops at a lower quality bar'. A single-field mutation test over every semantic field of C1-C7 guards its return. One recorded departure: a consumes reorder reads 'Other technical changes', because image order changes what the model sees (ADR-0098 decision 5).
- **Records:** ADR-0098 decisions 5-6 (projection, describers, presentation, the consumes-order departure), ADR-0092 decision 1 (FIELD_ROLES) and decision 5 (re-admission at start, 409), ADR-0097 decision 6 (explainers read promptShows), ADR-0100 item 12 (reads is ordered), docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md row F08
- **Tests:** packages/advisor/test/diff-semantics.test.ts 'every single-field change of a semantic field gets a line (FIELD_ROLES, C1–C7)' (never 'The same steps', never quotes the bar); 'what changes that are not semantic read' (renames read 'The same steps'); 'the verification's cases each read what changed' (random pick, AI pick, threshold 9->1, plateau/probe, Q1, V3, V3c, route question, optional trigger, critic into audit, K1-C1, K2b, retry D2->D1); packages/recipe/test/projection.test.ts 'FIELD_ROLES classifies exactly the schema's fields'; packages/advisor/test/present.test.ts 'a random pick: chosen at random...', 'a claim log is your sign-off on the claims...', 'a step labelled VOICE that writes no person's words is neither labelled nor explained as your words', 'a fix applies the critique by what it is, whatever its writes flag says'; apps/web/test/start-admission.test.ts 'a recipe whose explanation changed does not start (409)'; Ran in clone: all passed; the review's two F08 probes re-run now describe the change

#### F09: C7 collects clarification that its implementer does not consume (byway-review.md:132, 241-249; sub-review engine-protocol-review.md E3, lines 53-59; probe engine-probes.json c7Brief)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P2 (review P2), low end. (Verdict row line 36; section 2793-2834; options 2957-3053; recommendation 3055-3105; steps 3211-3216; acceptance 3231-3236; how-it-resolves 3323-3335; adversarial changes 3375-3401; final check residual 3413)
- **Root cause:** Two wrong assumptions at two boundaries (verification lines 2825-2833): (1) the recipe IR has only hard inputs, so `consumes` must be in `must` on every path (scope.ts ST5); a port that only an optional branch makes cannot reach a later step, so the catalog dropped `brief` from implement; (2) a human react step that consumes and produces the same text port is treated as writing it from blank: no initial text, the generic VISION-lite question, and the answer replaces the interview's numbered questions and defaults (interpreter.ts:416, wording.ts:30-31). A test gap hid both: the C7 e2e declined the interview and nothing asserted the brief reached implement.
- **Wider than reviewed:** (a) Even wired, the brief would hold bare answers ('3: keep the CSV header') with no questions and none of the defaults the person kept, because the human step replaces the port (lines 2802-2805); (b) the checkpoint asks the generic VISION-lite question, not the interview's questions; (c) the blind product.c7.scope-cut sees only the diff and its 'Why:', so it could list brief-mandated behaviour as unneeded (plausible, not reproduced); (d) the 'Trim the scope' explainer (presentation.ts:436) claims the reviewer knows what the task did not ask for; (e) the presentation said 'Your turn: answer them in a short spec' while nothing used it (line 2801).
- **Refuted or overstated:** The review puts the root cause at the catalog wiring; the verification puts it in the IR and validator (line 2808). The review's warning that requiring a port an optional may never produce 'would introduce another bug' is wrong: the validator refuses it before it ships, and at run time an absent port already renders as '' (line 2809). The demand that the brief reach 'subsequent revisions' was not reproduced and was narrowed: the remedy is not to feed the blind passes (C7-DR7) but to have the implementer's 'Why:' name the brief's requirements (line 2810, 3383-3385). Option B's synthetic skip-path brief was rejected (lines 2995, 3306).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Both causes the verification named are removed on main. (1) IR: `Step.reads` (packages/recipe/src/schema.ts:38-58, inputsOf) is typed in validator/src/scope.ts:348-361 (ST5 clauses reads-unmade, reads-twice, reads-actor; a read never enters `must`); PR1/PR2/PR3/PR5/PR7.1 read inputsOf; toStepSpec carries reads (runner/src/types.ts:158) and buildPrompt walks inputsOf (runner/src/activities/prompting.ts:144), filling an absent read with ''. C7's implement is `build('c7','implement',['intent'],{ reads: ['brief'] })` (catalog/src/c7.ts); recipes/c7.json carries reads and hash sha256:cefc7ab5... as ADR-0089 states; the coverage-test exemption is gone; research-frozen recipes are held to no reads. (2) Human checkpoint: revisesInPlace() (runner/src/workflow/wording.ts:34) and interpreter.ts:401-405 open the C7 spec checkpoint with the interview's text as initialText and ask SPEC_IN_PLACE_PROMPT; the answer replaces the port, so questions, answers and kept defaults reach implement. product.c7.implement v3 shows the spec under {{#if brief}} and asks the 'Why:' to name its requirements; the blind passes never get the brief (C7-DR7). 'Your direction' and 'Trim the scope' copy corrected (presentation.ts). Admission semantics also learned the shape (ADR-0098 decision text at line 112, commit 6dca680). Tests that would catch a return: I ran them in a clone of 8401820 and they pass. Two mutations were each caught: making buildPrompt iterate only `consumes` fails runner/test/reads.test.ts, and removing the prefill fails the C7 interview e2e.
- **Records:** ADR-0089 (Decision 1-7; Consequences: closes backlog Product follow-ups row 1), ADR-0090 Decision §1 (a person's reaction revises its port in place), ADR-0098 decision 5 (amends ADR-0089 decision 1: reads is ordered semantic) and its line-112 rule on revise-in-place answers, ADR-0100 item 12 (correction to ADR-0089 decision 1), docs/adr/README.md row ADR-0089 'changed by 0098, 0100'
- **Tests:** packages/validator/test/reads.test.ts (9 cases incl. 'consuming that port instead is refused', 'a read never makes a port required', 'only a model step reads optionally', PR1/PR5, PR3/PR7.1, first-round loop limit); packages/recipe/test/reads.test.ts ('leave the hash of a recipe that does not use them as it was'); packages/runner/test/reads.test.ts ('fills the read port's slot with its value when an earlier step made it, and empty when none did'; caught mutation M1); packages/runner/test/revise-in-place.test.ts ('in the catalog only C7's spec does', question wording); packages/prompts/test/coverage.test.ts (exemption removed), packages/prompts/test/step-slots.test.ts; packages/prompts/test/media-contracts.test.ts 'C7 implement (F09)': with a spec the Why: names its requirements; without one neither; packages/catalog/test/configurations.test.ts (C7), packages/catalog/test/conformance.test.ts (research-frozen recipes use no reads); packages/runner/test/e2e/text-media.ts via runner/test/configurations.test.ts: 'with the interview: you answer its questions in place, and implement works from the whole spec (F09)' (offered === questions, brief holds Q1 default and Q2 answer, the provider request carries both, blind passes get no brief; caught mutation M7) and the declined case (slots.brief === '', run done)

#### F10: C5's shot critic observes the storyboard/still instead of the actual clip (byway-review.md:133, 251-259; sub-review engine-protocol-review.md E4, lines 61-69; probe engine-probes.json c5VideoReview)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT; severity P2 (review P2). (Verdict row line 37; section 2836-2878; options 2957-3053; recommendation 3061, 3165-3190; steps 3217-3227; acceptance 3237-3242; how-it-resolves 3336-3343; adversarial changes 3386-3396; final check residual 3411)
- **Root cause:** Render is never told which relay link it renders, and the clip's own frames are never bound to its shot: the media view was defined once as 'whole storyboard text plus each shot's pre-chain ASSET still'; relayLink recorded clipArtifactId but not the last frame the gateway already returned; the interpreter knew ports.shot and linkStart but did not pass them to render. The check-clean loop also had no 'could not observe' outcome, so missing evidence became 'No defects.' or futile repairs (lines 2867-2877). The verification withdrew 'the gateway contract carries images only' as a cause (line 2875, 3391).
- **Wider than reviewed:** (a) The check is not scoped to its shot: shot 1's and shot 2's checks were byte-identical; (b) regenerations cannot change the checker's input, so a flaw in any still drove paid regenerations up to the cap question (9 video calls instead of 3); (c) with no video key the check still ran on stills and reported 'No defects.' for clips never made (lines 2848-2851, 2858); (d) inherited defects visible in the start frame would drive futile regenerations (line 3392); (e) the advisor estimate still counts shot-check calls without video (line 3273, 3402).
- **Refuted or overstated:** None of the review's claims; its line references are accurate (line 2858). The verification said the review understates the impact. Its own first design (middle-frame sampling inside the billed video call) was withdrawn after the critics, because a required extraction adds a billed-but-fatal failure point (lines 3386-3390).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** The verification's root cause is removed on main. relayLink writes clipArtifactId and lastFrameArtifactId together (runner/src/activities/relay.ts:100); the interpreter passes link {shot, startFrame} to render only while a link runs (interpreter.ts:312), and relay() clears shot/linkStart on exit (interpreter.ts:782). render.ts linkView (lines 93-106) shows only that shot's line plus 'The frame this clip starts from:' and 'The last frame of the generated clip:', or returns `unobservable` with noClipNote when the clip or last frame is missing. unobservable() (interpreter.ts:794-799) skips a model check or critic only inside a link whose view has nothing to judge, and the loop exits 'unchecked' (interpreter.ts:301, 573). product.c5.shot-check v2 says it sees the line, the start frame and the last frame, reports only defects the clip introduced, and says motion cannot be seen; the Motion explainer says the same. The review's 'observe motion' option is not built; it took the 'explicitly limit' branch: the check's wording is limited (not renamed), and middle-frame sampling is in the backlog. Tests passed in the clone. Mutations were caught: render ignoring link fails three link-view tests (M3); a judge asked with nothing to see fails the C5 no-video e2e (M8). The final-check residual about the rule being 'worded backwards' (line 3411) did not reach code: the guard returns undefined when ports.shot is undefined, and the no-video e2e shows the launch-gate critic after the chain still called twice.
- **Records:** ADR-0090 Decision §3 (the shot check sees its clip) and Consequences, ADR-0093 decision 6 ('unchecked' is not a cap exit and takes no cap turn), ADR-0098 line 168 (flow 'exit' after a judge skipped in a relay link), ADR-0079 RENDER-1 (principle applied), ADR-0069 changed by ADR-0090 (index row)
- **Tests:** packages/runner/test/link-view.test.ts: 'sees exactly the frame its clip started from and the clip's last frame, under their captions, and that shot's line'; 'two clips with different last frames send different checks'; 'records the clip's last frame on its shot, beside the clip'; 'a shot with no clip has nothing to check, and says why'; 'outside a link the view is the whole storyboard'; 'the shot-check prompt says exactly what it is shown and what it cannot see'; 'the Motion explainer says the same'; packages/runner/test/e2e/c2-c5.ts via configurations.test.ts: main C5 run (each shot-check attaches exactly [start frame, that clip's last frame]; no regenerate step started; first video starts from shot 1's still); 'with keys but no video model, every relay link finishes skipped...' (zero shot-check calls, each skipped with noClipNote, no regenerate, no loop-iteration, no 'Use the best version so far?', gate-critic still called twice); packages/runner/test/loop-cap.test.ts (an unchecked round at the cap asks nothing; the same loop that sees its clip asks the cap question), per ADR-0093 Evidence

| Claim | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Renderer ignores clipArtifactId and shows storyboard text plus stills | fixed | render.ts linkView uses shot.lastFrameArtifactId and link.startFrame; relay.ts:100 records lastFrameArtifactId; commit 4a66719; mutation M3 caught |

| View is the whole storyboard, not the current shot | fixed | linkView text is that shot's line only; c2-c5 e2e asserts each check attaches only its own start and last frame |

#### F11: C3 prompt allows no illustration while runtime requires one (byway-review.md:134, 261-269; sub-review engine-protocol-review.md E5, lines 71-79; probe engine-extra-probes.json noImages)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P3 (review P2); borderline P2 only on the worst case. (Verdict row line 38; section 2880-2915; options 2957-3053; recommendation 3063, 3106-3163; steps 3194-3210; acceptance 3243-3247; how-it-resolves 3344-3351; adversarial changes 3361-3372; final check residual 3412, 3414; §4 decision 3 line 91)
- **Root cause:** Two wrong assumptions (lines 2908-2914): (1) the runtime decided image cardinality by medium (checkBriefs: at least one, at most the slots knob, via Math.max(1, cap)) while each prompt states its own count, so there were two definitions that disagreed; (2) a well-formed short answer had no honest degraded outcome: askModel's single repair retry turned a second short answer into a fatal ContractMissed, though assetStep already had a pass-through convention (asset.ts:97-99). Separately, C3's prompt gave the model a veto over the person's opt-in at the optional gate.
- **Wider than reviewed:** The fatal short-answer outcome applies to every minimum-1 ASSET step, not only C3 (e.g. C1/C1-H article.ASSET empty twice). A per-medium 'zero allowed on prose and web-page' rule would be wrong: the reachable prompts there ask for at least one, and cardinality belongs to the prompt (line 2897). The same 'optional enhancement fails late' class covers asset.integrate's 'Place every image' miss after purchase (parked, line 3311). The C1-H twice-empty path changes behaviour and must be disclosed as an arm deviation (line 3281, 3414).
- **Refuted or overstated:** (1) A run failure requires the model to return empty twice, ignoring explicit corrective feedback; the typical outcome is one extra mid-tier call and then the image (line 2893). (2) helper@prose is not reached by canonical or composed recipes (line 2894). (3) 'Unwanted image purchase' is overstated for C3: the step sits behind optional('asset-if-wanted'), so the person already said yes; the prompt's empty-list line is a model veto, not a protected creative decision (line 2895). The review's 'empty is success on prose' keyed on medium is wrong (line 2998, 3361).
- **Resolution (decided and built):** The two causes were fixed in ADR-0090 without waiting for D3, and D3 is now decided and built. (1) The count belongs to the prompt: every image-briefs template declares `images:` (template.ts requires it there and refuses it elsewhere); the renderer returns RenderedPrompt.images, and assetStep builds the free plan from it inside the parse callback (asset.ts:122-123, slot-plan.ts freePlan). That also settles the final-check ordering residual (line 3412). Declarations on main: article.ASSET 1-3, helper@web-page 1-3, helper@prose 0-3, product.c3.asset 1-1 (v3), each on media and options. (2) A short answer degrades: askModel returns the usable part with a shortfall after one retry (call.ts:76-99), and only malformed answers throw ContractMissed. A min-0 prompt answering zero finishes not skipped with NO_IMAGE_CHOSEN_NOTE; zero usable briefs finish skipped with NO_BRIEF_NOTE, so a deliberate no-image is told apart from a failed provider. D3 (ADR-0101, option B+): product.c3.asset v3 has no empty-list line and declares 1-1 plus `advice: yes`. An empty C3 answer is asked again once; empty twice, the step is skipped and the run goes on. The AI's view becomes one cleaned advice line in the note (ADR-0104 tightened what counts as advice). Tests passed in the clone. Two mutations were caught: ignoring the declared range (M5) and restoring the throw on shortfall (M9, 9 tests fail, including 'C3 answered empty twice ... (no ContractMissed)').
- **Records:** ADR-0090 Decision §2 (the ASSET brief contract; 'C3 keeps its no image answer (owner decision pending)' later replaced), ADR-0101 (D3, owner chose B+ 2026-10-16; replaces ADR-0090 §2's C3 bullet, the 0-1 declaration and the explainer), ADR-0104 (advice must hold a letter or number; later notes 2026-10-18), ADR-0102 Decision 2 (the image step no longer retries a declined image), docs/backlog.md 'Owner decisions from the external-review verification': D3 decided (ADR-0101), docs/reviews/2026-10-16-review-fix-status.md row F11
- **Tests:** packages/prompts/test/media-contracts.test.ts: 'every image-briefs template declares it', 'a template offers an empty list exactly when its minimum is 0', 'refuses a count on any other template...', 'v3: says the writer asked for the image and asks for one brief, with no way to answer with none', 'only C3's header-image prompt asks for one' (advice); packages/runner/test/asset-slots.test.ts: 'C3: your Yes decides. An empty answer is asked again...', 'C3 answered empty twice: nothing is bought, the step is skipped with its note, and the run goes on (no ContractMissed)', 'the generic prose helper may answer with no image too', 'C1's ASSET answered empty twice ... (no ContractMissed)', 'a malformed answer, twice, still fails the step', 'the harness's fake prompts carry the count each real image-briefs template declares', advice tests, 'every step whose prompt asks for advice has a Trim step after it'; packages/runner/test/formats.test.ts (advice is one plain bounded line, never a miss); packages/runner/test/e2e/text-media.ts via configurations.test.ts: 'your Yes decides: an empty brief is asked for again; empty twice, the step is skipped with its note and the run goes on (D3, ADR-0101)' and advice in the note

| Claim | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Prompt permits empty while runtime requires one | fixed | One definition: the template's `images:` read through RenderedPrompt.images (slot-plan.ts freePlan, asset.ts:123). C3 v3 declares 1-1 with no empty-list line (ADR-0101); media-contracts.test.ts holds the empty-list wording to min 0 |
| A compliant no-image answer ends in two calls then ContractMissed | fixed | Empty twice now finishes skipped with NO_BRIEF_NOTE and the run continues: asset-slots.test.ts 'C3 answered empty twice ... (no ContractMissed)'; text-media.ts e2e; mutation M9 caught |
| helper.asset.briefs@prose permits empty | fixed (and noted unreachable) | helper@prose declares images: 0-3; asset-slots.test.ts 'the generic prose helper may answer with no image too' (NO_IMAGE_CHOSEN_NOTE, not skipped); verification line 2894 says it is unreachable from canonical or composed recipes |

| No-image as an explicit successful no-op that records what was skipped | fixed for min-0 prompts; superseded for C3 by D3 | NO_IMAGE_CHOSEN_NOTE (finished not skipped) for min-0 prompts; for C3, ADR-0101 makes Yes decide and keeps the AI's view as advice only |
| Do not repair a valid creative decision into an unwanted purchase | refuted for C3 (overstated); owner decided | Verification line 2895: the person opted in at the optional gate. Owner decision D3 (ADR-0101): 'A wrong image is cheap to undo at Trim'; the AI may only advise |

#### F12: Media slots validate one representation and attach using another (byway-review.md:135, 271-279; sub-review engine-protocol-review.md E5, lines 71-79; probes engine-extra-probes.json whitespaceSlot, missingFirstSlot)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P2 (review P2), low end, driven by the anchor substitution; the whitespace, alias and duplicate parts alone are P3. (Verdict row line 39; section 2917-2955; options 2957-3053; recommendation 3063, 3128-3161; steps 3194-3210; acceptance 3248-3253; how-it-resolves 3352-3357; adversarial changes 3366-3374, 3397)
- **Root cause:** assetStep has no single typed slot identity, no declared coverage rule and no honest outcome when coverage falls short (lines 2949-2954): parse keeps the raw string and validate, dedupe, attach, announce and the anchor sort each use a different form (trimmed/uppercased, raw, untrimmed uppercase, Number()); coverage is 'at least one matches'; per-slot failures are swallowed; the anchor is whichever image was made, not the storyboard's first shot; the relay has no outcome for 'no start frame', so it either substitutes another shot's still or fails on fal's invalid-request.
- **Wider than reviewed:** (a) Per-slot gateway failures that survive retries are swallowed (asset.ts:93), and anchor-frames runs with tries=1 (asset.ts:56), so a failed shot-1 image (not only an omitted brief) makes shot 2's still the start frame of shot 1's paid clip, and its regenerations reuse it (relay.ts:130, interpreter.ts:694), against ADR-0069 and spec §3.2/§3.3; (b) with no anchor at all, relayLink calls video with no start frame and fal's invalid-request fails the run (fal.ts:142); (c) an unparseable storyboard on the generic path bought images and then attach() threw (line 3374); (d) '1' plus ' 1' bought 4 images for 3 shots (line 2926).
- **Refuted or overstated:** The review's one-line root cause ('validate one representation, attach another') covers only the whitespace/alias half; the consequential half is the missing coverage rule and the anchor taken from whichever image was made (line 2937). In the whitespace case the image is not wasted: it is announced as shot 1 and is still the right anchor; only the stored still is missing (line 2938). Whitespace and alias slots are improbable from real models (line 2939). Option B's coverage-as-hard-parse-failure was rejected because it re-creates F11's late failure (line 2999).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** slot-plan.ts on main is the one owner of slot identity, coverage and the anchor. eachPlan reads the storyboard (shots sorted ascending, first = lowest) or option labels before any call, and throws a StepError on unreadable work. slotId trims and canonicalizes ('Shot 1', ' 1 ', '01' -> '1'; 'a', 'Option A' -> 'A'), and checking, deduplication (last wins), buying, attaching, announcing and the anchor all use it. checkBriefs treats a missing expected slot as a miss carrying the usable briefs, so askModel retries once and then degrades: covered slots are bought, and the note names missing, unknown and failed slots. assetStep gives shot 1's frame at least two tries, records failed slots, and sets the anchor only to the image whose slot is plan.first (asset.ts:208-210). relayLink skips a link whose start is not an image, before any model or video call (relay.ts:45-46, noStartFrameNote). storyboardShots sorts the same way, so the chain order and `first` agree. Later changes kept the identity rules: ADR-0102 means a declined image is not retried by the step, even for shot 1; ADR-0099/ADR-0090 §4 mean a billed-but-not-kept image stops the run with a message naming what was billed. Tests passed in the clone. Mutations were caught: anchor = first image made (M4, 4 tests fail) and slot identity = raw string (M6, 2 tests fail).
- **Records:** ADR-0090 Decision §2 (one slot plan; a short answer degrades; the relay anchor is shot 1's frame or none) and §4 (an image billed but not kept is not bought again), ADR-0099 decision 5 (billed failures stop the step), ADR-0102 Decision 2 (the image step no longer retries a declined image; amends ADR-0090's 'a refusal is tried once more'), ADR-0069 changed by ADR-0090 (relay clause amended by addition)
- **Tests:** packages/runner/test/asset-slots.test.ts 'one slot identity on media (C5 anchor frames)': 'a shot number with spaces around it attaches to its shot'; 'two spellings of one shot buy one image, and "Shot 1" is shot 1'; 'a brief list missing shot 1 is asked again before anything is bought; still missing, the rest is bought, the note names shot 1, and there is no anchor'; 'when shot 1's image is refused on both tries, there is no anchor and the note names shot 1'; 'out of order, the anchor is still shot 1's frame'; 'a storyboard that cannot be read fails the step before any call'; asset-slots.test.ts 'one slot identity on options (C2 mood boards)': 'labels in either case buy one image each'; 'a label that is not an option and a missing one: asked again, then the rest is bought and the note names both'; asset-slots.test.ts 'a relay link with no frame to start from (F12)': 'is skipped plainly before any model or video call, instead of starting a paid clip from another shot's frame'; asset-slots.test.ts 'the slot plan on its own' and round-2 groups (covers-every-slot, short-then-empty, short-then-malformed); billed-image group; packages/runner/test/e2e/c2-c5.ts via configurations.test.ts: the first video request's startFrameArtifactId is shot 1's imageArtifactId
