#### R4: Define a local backup/recovery acceptance scenario (release action 4)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT; severity P3. (51 (verdict row), 4465-4482, 4707-4722 (design item 5), 4772-4778 (steps 5-6), 4791-4793, 4803, 4830-4831, 4857-4860, 4877-4879)
- **Root cause:** Authoritative state is split across independently written stores: SQLite in WAL mode, CAS files referenced from SQLite, a JSONL ledger with its .head, the exposure register, and Temporal's SQLite. Nothing defines which set of files, in which state, is a backup. The one written statement was wrong and never exercised. Start-up reconciliation covers only the store-ahead-of-Temporal direction, and the ledger treats a missing file as zero spend (verification:4482).
- **Wider than reviewed:** ADR-0066:26's 'one file to back up' is wrong: with the store open, a byway.sqlite copy alone has no tables, because commits sit in the WAL (probe P3). The persisted set also includes exposure.jsonl, ledger.jsonl.head and the WAL/SHM sidecars. A missing or rolled-back ledger resets spend, so calls over the cap are admitted (P1, P5); verifyLedger() returns null on a missing file and had no production caller. Missing artifact files were found only on read. A store older than Temporal was unhandled. Temporal ahead of the store on the same run hangs silently (verification:4469-4476).
- **Refuted or overstated:** reconcileLostRuns scanning only the default workspace is not a v1 gap, because the Store contract has no createWorkspace or listWorkspaces. No incident was shown (4478).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Each part of the cause is addressed. ADR-0094 defines the backup unit: every file in BYWAY_DATA_DIR, copied with byway stopped and the engine confirmed gone (port and process check). A restore replaces the whole directory. This corrects ADR-0066, and the index links them. The README 'Back up and restore' section gives Windows and Linux commands and names what each mix does. scripts/test/backup.test.ts shows that a single-file copy taken while the store is open lacks committed data, and that a whole-directory copy reopens intact. It also requires the README to name every file byway writes. ADR-0096 adds checkStore (quick_check, missing or wrong-size artifact files with the runs that use them) and dataDirReport, printed at start before the engine. It also makes reconcileLostRuns a two-way pass that terminates orphan run workflows with ORPHAN_RUN_MESSAGE before the worker polls. F03's spend book in byway.sqlite (ADR-0095 decision 9) took cap authority away from the JSONL ledger, so a lost or older ledger no longer resets the caps. The two F03 restore stubs became plain tests (082bebe), and the README caveat was removed with that fail-open (ADR-0100 item 15). The split stores remain by design; the likely bad mixes are now detected or made harmless. The engine-ahead-of-store-on-the-same-run mix remains undetected, as a recorded limit.
- **Records:** ADR-0094 (backup unit; corrects ADR-0066), ADR-0096 (start-up report; two-way store/engine pass), ADR-0095 decision 9 (restores no longer reset caps), ADR-0100 items 15-17 (later corrections to ADR-0094/0096), README 'Back up and restore' and the Data table, spec §2 Data (ace231e)
- **Tests:** scripts/test/backup.test.ts: 'a copy of byway.sqlite alone, taken while byway runs, lacks what the store has committed'; 'a copy of the whole directory, taken after byway stops, reopens with the same records, artifacts, spend and exposure'; 'the Data table names every file byway writes...'; 'a "Back up and restore" section gives the whole-directory procedure...'; 'points to ADR-0094, which corrects ADR-0066'; packages/store/test/check.test.ts: 'names a missing artifact file and every run whose records use it'; 'names a file that is not the size the store recorded'; 'only reads...'; packages/runner/test/restore.test.ts: 'a whole-directory copy ... reopens intact, and the report finds nothing'; 'names the calls ledger.jsonl lacks after a restore without it and its head...'; 'names them after a restore of an older ledger and head pair too'; 'caps after a restore that lost the ledger': 'without ledger.jsonl and its head, a call the original spend puts over the run cap is refused', 'with an older ledger and head pair, ... refused'; packages/runner/test/engine-state.test.ts: the orphan run is terminated with ORPHAN_RUN_MESSAGE at engine start; Ran in my clone: backup, check, restore and engine-state all pass

| Claim | Status | Evidence |

#### R5: Retain accessibility work and test the interactive boundaries (release action 5)

- **Verification:** ACCEPT; severity P3. (52 (verdict row), 4485-4507, 4724-4746 (design item 6), 4779-4782 (steps 7-8), 4794-4798, 4807, 4832-4834, 4861-4866, 4875, 4884, 4890-4891 (final-check residuals), 4895)
- **Root cause:** Accessibility is built into components as static attributes, and nothing exercises state transitions in a real browser with the keyboard. 'An action in flight' has no shared owner: each component implements busy and unavailable states on its own, mostly by handing both focus behaviour and double-submit protection to the DOM `disabled` attribute. So RunScreen's correct aria-disabled-plus-guard pattern was not reused, and nothing stops `disabled` from coming back (verification:4507).
- **Wider than reviewed:** Self-disabling controls go beyond the four panels: SelectPanel's 'Choose all' and 'Clear', StartForm:207, RecommendationView:428 (the paid 'Start with $X') and the Examples file input. On StartForm and the paid Start button, `disabled` was the only double-submit guard, with no server idempotency (client.ts:68-93). Closing StepDetail dropped focus to BODY. The C1 compare view rendered both pages aria-hidden and tabindex=-1, with no Open link (moved to F13 as P2) (verification:4492-4503).
- **Refuted or overstated:** None. The defects R5 surfaced are owned by F05, F06, F07 and F13.
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Action-in-flight state now has one shared owner, apps/web/src/components/useAction.ts plus ActionButton.tsx. A synchronous guard refuses a second start before React re-renders, and controls stay focusable with aria-disabled and aria-busy. Every self-disabling or busy control is converted: the panels, SelectPanel's bulk buttons, Start, the paid Start, Build my own and the Examples file input. guard-disabled.test.ts fails any native `disabled` in apps/web src/ and app/ outside three reviewed entries (Examples fieldset, 'Paste an example', Switch), and fails a stale entry. The rule is written in the apps/web README Guardrails. RunScreen returns focus to the timeline row that opened a step. e2e/keyboard.spec.ts drives these transitions with the keyboard in a real browser, through pnpm e2e and CI. The cross-cluster stubs for F05, F06, F07 and F13 became plain tests once those fixes landed. Beyond the verification's 'no server idempotency' choice, ADR-0099 decision 7 later added run-start idempotency on the server. The final-check residual about a blocked Start is closed: RecommendationView start() returns when blocked (:391), and ActionButton has unavailable={blocked} (:514).
- **Records:** apps/web README Guardrails 'A control that acts never disables itself (R5)' (:118-139), spec §5 keyboard and focus (1f68b48), useAction.ts header comment, ADR-0099 decision 7 (server-side run-start idempotency; ADR-0100 'Outside the ADRs'), backlog a11y rows (4b4d344)
- **Tests:** apps/web/test/guard-disabled.test.ts: 'the disabled scan finds the attribute on any intrinsic element...'; 'does not count aria-disabled, ... a component’s prop...'; 'native disabled state in apps/web (R5) is used only where reviewed'; apps/web/test/action.test.ts: 'refuses a second start while the first runs, in the same tick, before any re-render'; 'is marked unavailable with aria-disabled, and busy with aria-busy, never with disabled'; 'renders as a focusable button...'; apps/web/e2e/keyboard.spec.ts: 'Yes on a confirm, pressed with Enter twice...'; 'Send reaction, pressed with Enter twice; the words stay'; '“Choose all” and “Clear” keep focus...'; 'Start, the paid Start button, Build my own and the examples’ image input'; 'Start: each attachment added by keyboard takes focus in its name field...'; 'closing it with Escape or Back returns focus to the row that opened it'; '[F06] ...'; '[F05] the viewer’s scripts switch is reached with Tab, named, and turned on and off with Space'; '[F13 compare view] ...'; '[F07] after a nav link of a page is edited, every link on the page still has a name'; Ran in my clone: guard-disabled and action tests pass. keyboard.spec was not run locally; it is in CI's pnpm e2e, green on main at 8401820 (run 39177268735)

| Claim | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Static markup checks cannot certify the interaction | fixed | keyboard.spec.ts uses page.keyboard.press with focus assertions (expectFocus, focusedDescription) and accessibility-tree names (a11y.ts expectNamedControls), run by CI |
| Long decision views | fixed | '[F13 compare view] each version ... is a named picture of its first screen, said so, and opens whole from the keyboard' (F13 fixed: ADR-0086, be611f7) |

#### RS: Research prerequisites and the blanket 'external seed strings untested' wording

- **Verification:** ACCEPT_PARTLY; severity P3 (wording); none (activation requirements). (53 (verdict row), 4510-4525, 4700-4705 (design item 4), 4758-4763 (item 7), 4771, 4783, 4799, 4835-4836, 4867, 4876, 4892 (final-check residual))
- **Root cause:** Summary surfaces (map GAPS and SEED evidence, 04's findings and gaps, the README prescription list) compress the block prose into slogans. They have no link back to the corrected text or to the protocol's Corrections log, so the earlier correction at 01-blocks:97 never reached them. Separately, contract comments promise registry behaviour (expiry degradation) that no code or test enforces (verification:4525).
- **Wider than reviewed:** The blanket wording is on five surfaces: map:675, map:393, protocol/04:221, 04:200 and README:130. 01-blocks:97 was already narrow but omitted D.6. The recipe hash covers only recipe JSON. STUDY_REGISTRY is empty. labelFor ignores `expires`, while contracts/advisor.ts:49 promised degradation to 'Historical', which the verdict union cannot represent. Spec §3.3's runner-injected string (model-step.ts:30) is D.6's Seed Injection form (4514-4521).
- **Refuted or overstated:** The activation requirements restate the accepted charter ADRs (0041, 0054, 0055) and existing backlog items, and they are inert while the registry is empty and the research track is parked. They are down-scoped to severity none (4521-4523).
- **Resolution (root cause fixed):** Wording: 12a4bda narrows the claim on all five surfaces. The summary lines now link to 01 · SEED. 01-blocks names §5.1.2 (probabilistic instruction following) and App. D.6 (DeepSeek-R1-0528 on NoveltyBench Curated) and says the article's shell-seeded design workflow is untested. Every changed statement has a Round 3 row in protocol/04 #Corrections (:271-280). scripts/test/seed-evidence.test.ts fails on the original phrases or blanket rewordings on any protocol surface, requires a Round 3 row per original, requires 01's D.6 citation with model and benchmark, and checks that the links resolve. The final check's two notes are closed: the test lists map:393's and README:130's phrasings, and the corrected rows tell §5.1.2 and D.6 apart. Contract: 6859203 makes advisor.ts's `expires` comment say nothing acts on it yet and point to the backlog gate. label.test.ts shows an expired entry keeps its verdict, and its empty-registry tripwire message names that gate. The research prerequisites are severity none and inert, so they are filed with a tripwire, not built. The guard is specific to the seed claim; a future correction to another block's prose has no test tying its summaries to it.
- **Records:** protocol/04-use-case-evidence.md Corrections 'Round 3: post-release review (2026-10-08)', protocol/01-blocks.md:97 (SEED evidence), spec 'SEED in the product' note (§3.3), packages/contracts/src/advisor.ts:49-53 comment, backlog 'Before the study registry's first entry', backlog Deferred decisions 'SEED string source in the product'
- **Tests:** scripts/test/seed-evidence.test.ts: 'no protocol surface says external seed strings are untested'; '01 · SEED cites App. D.6 with its model and benchmark beside §5.1.2, and keeps the article's setup untested'; 'every corrected statement has a Round 3 row in 04 · Corrections...'; 'the corrected surfaces' links resolve'; 'the product spec's SEED note cites D.6 as indirect evidence about the runner-injected string'; packages/advisor/test/label.test.ts: 'the v1 registry is empty' (tripwire naming the backlog gate) and the expired-entry case; Ran in my clone: pass
